"use client";
import { useState } from "react";

export default function PaperTradingPage() {
  const API_BASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL;

  // Form state
  const [tickerList, setTickerList] = useState("");
  const [timeInterval, setTimeInterval] = useState("5Min");
  const [drlLib, setDrlLib] = useState("elegantrl");
  const [agent, setAgent] = useState("ppo");
  const [cwd, setCwd] = useState("./papertrading_crypto");
  const [netDim, setNetDim] = useState("128,64");
  const [stateDim, setStateDim] = useState(22);
  const [actionDim, setActionDim] = useState(3);
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState("");
  const [apiSecret, setApiSecret] = useState("");
  const [apiBaseUrl, setApiBaseUrl] = useState("https://paper-api.alpaca.markets");
  const [techIndicatorList, setTechIndicatorList] = useState("");
  const [maxStock, setMaxStock] = useState(100.0);

  // Response & error
  const [response, setResponse] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setResponse(null);
    setError(null);

    // Convert comma-separated strings
    const tickersArray = tickerList.split(",").map((s) => s.trim());
    const netDimArray = netDim.split(",").map((s) => Number(s.trim()));
    const techArray = techIndicatorList.split(",").map((s) => s.trim());

    const payload = {
      ticker_list: tickersArray,
      time_interval: timeInterval,
      drl_lib: drlLib,
      agent: agent,
      cwd: cwd,
      net_dim: netDimArray,
      state_dim: Number(stateDim),
      action_dim: Number(actionDim),
      API_KEY: apiKey,
      API_SECRET: apiSecret,
      API_BASE_URL: apiBaseUrl,
      tech_indicator_list: techArray,
      max_stock: Number(maxStock),
    };

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/paper_trade`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${res.status}`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      setResponse(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="text-white" style={{ fontFamily: 'Poppins, sans-serif' }}>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Paper Trading Form</h1>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        {/* Ticker List */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">
            Ticker List (comma-separated)
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={tickerList}
            onChange={(e) => setTickerList(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="BTCUSD,ETHUSD,SOLUSD"
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Time Interval */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Time Interval</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={timeInterval}
            onChange={(e) => setTimeInterval(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* DRL Lib */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">DRL Lib</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={drlLib}
            onChange={(e) => setDrlLib(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Agent */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Agent</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={agent}
            onChange={(e) => setAgent(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* CWD */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">CWD</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={cwd}
            onChange={(e) => setCwd(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Net Dim */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Net Dim (comma-separated)</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={netDim}
            onChange={(e) => setNetDim(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* State Dim */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">State Dim</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={stateDim}
            onChange={(e) => setStateDim(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Action Dim */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Action Dim</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={actionDim}
            onChange={(e) => setActionDim(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* API Key */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">API Key</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={apiKey}
            onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)}
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* API Secret */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">API Secret</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={apiSecret}
            onChange={(e) => setApiSecret(e.target.value)}
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* API Base URL */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">API Base URL</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={apiBaseUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setApiBaseUrl(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Tech Indicator List */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">
            Tech Indicator List (comma-separated)
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={techIndicatorList}
            onChange={(e) => setTechIndicatorList(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="macd,rsi,cci,dx"
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Max Stock */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Max Stock</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={maxStock}
            onChange={(e) => setMaxStock(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold rounded hover:bg-green-700"
        >
          Start Paper Trading
        </button>
      </form>

      {error && <p className="text-red-500 mt-4">Error: {error}</p>}
      {response && (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">Response:</h3>
          <pre className="bg-gray-100 p-4 rounded mt-2">
            {JSON.stringify(response, null, 2)}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

});

});

};

```typescript
"use client";
import { useState } from "react";

export default function PaperTradingPage() {
  const API_BASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL;

  // Form state
  const [tickerList, setTickerList] = useState("");
  const [timeInterval, setTimeInterval] = useState("5Min");
  const [drlLib, setDrlLib] = useState("elegantrl");
  const [agent, setAgent] = useState("ppo");
  const [cwd, setCwd] = useState("./papertrading_crypto");
  const [netDim, setNetDim] = useState("128,64");
  const [stateDim, setStateDim] = useState(22);
  const [actionDim, setActionDim] = useState(3);
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState("");
  const [apiSecret, setApiSecret] = useState("");
  const [apiBaseUrl, setApiBaseUrl] = useState("https://paper-api.alpaca.markets");
  const [techIndicatorList, setTechIndicatorList] = useState("");
  const [maxStock, setMaxStock] = useState(100.0);

  // Response & error
  const [response, setResponse] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setResponse(null);
    setError(null);

    // Convert comma-separated strings
    const tickersArray = tickerList.split(",").map((s) => s.trim());
    const netDimArray = netDim.split(",").map((s) => Number(s.trim()));
    const techArray = techIndicatorList.split(",").map((s) => s.trim());

    const payload = {
      ticker_list: tickersArray,
      time_interval: timeInterval,
      drl_lib: drlLib,
      agent: agent,
      cwd: cwd,
      net_dim: netDimArray,
      state_dim: Number(stateDim),
      action_dim: Number(actionDim),
      API_KEY: apiKey,
      API_SECRET: apiSecret,
      API_BASE_URL: apiBaseUrl,
      tech_indicator_list: techArray,
      max_stock: Number(maxStock),
    };

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/paper_trade`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${res.status}`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      setResponse(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: 'Poppins, sans-serif' }}>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Paper Trading Form</h1>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        {/* Ticker List */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">
            Ticker List (comma-separated)
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={tickerList}
            onChange={(e) => setTickerList(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="BTCUSD,ETHUSD,SOLUSD"
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Time Interval */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Time Interval</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={timeInterval}
            onChange={(e) => setTimeInterval(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* DRL Lib */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">DRL Lib</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={drlLib}
            onChange={(e) => setDrlLib(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Agent */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Agent</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={agent}
            onChange={(e) => setAgent(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* CWD */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">CWD</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={cwd}
            onChange={(e) => setCwd(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Net Dim */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Net Dim (comma-separated)</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={netDim}
            onChange={(e) => setNetDim(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* State Dim */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">State Dim</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={stateDim}
            onChange={(e) => setStateDim(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Action Dim */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Action Dim</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={actionDim}
            onChange={(e) => setActionDim(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* API Key */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">API Key</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={apiKey}
            onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)}
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* API Secret */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">API Secret</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={apiSecret}
            onChange={(e) => setApiSecret(e.target.value)}
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* API Base URL */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">API Base URL</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={apiBaseUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setApiBaseUrl(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Tech Indicator List */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">
            Tech Indicator List (comma-separated)
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={techIndicatorList}
            onChange={(e) => setTechIndicatorList(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="macd,rsi,cci,dx"
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Max Stock */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Max Stock</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={maxStock}
            onChange={(e) => setMaxStock(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold rounded hover:bg-green-700"
        >
          Start Paper Trading
        </button>
      </form>

      {error && <p className="text-red-500 mt-4">Error: {error}</p>}
      {response && (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">Response:</h3>
          <pre className="bg-gray-100 p-4 rounded mt-2">
            {JSON.stringify(response, null, 2)}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

"use client";
import { useState } from "react";

export default function TrainPage() {
  const API_BASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL;

  // Form state
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState("");
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState("");
  const [tickerList, setTickerList] = useState("");
  const [dataSource, setDataSource] = useState("binance");
  const [timeInterval, setTimeInterval] = useState("15Min");
  const [technicalIndicators, setTechnicalIndicators] = useState("");
  const [drlLib, setDrlLib] = useState("elegantrl");
  const [env, setEnv] = useState("CryptoTradingEnv");
  const [modelName, setModelName] = useState("ppo");
  const [cwd, setCwd] = useState("./papertrading_crypto");
  const [breakStep, setBreakStep] = useState(100000);

  const [response, setResponse] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setResponse(null);

    const tickersArray = tickerList.split(",").map((item) => item.trim());
    const techArray = technicalIndicators.split(",").map((item) => item.trim());

    const payload = {
      start_date: startDate,
      end_date: endDate,
      ticker_list: tickersArray,
      data_source: dataSource,
      time_interval: timeInterval,
      technical_indicator_list: techArray,
      drl_lib: drlLib,
      env: env,
      model_name: modelName,
      cwd: cwd,
      break_step: Number(breakStep),
    };

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/train`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${res.status}`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      setResponse(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message);
    }
  };

            onChange={(e) => setEndDate(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD"
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Ticker List */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Ticker List (comma-separated)</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={tickerList}
            onChange={(e) => setTickerList(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT"
            required
            className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Data Source */}
        <div>
          <label className="block font-medium mb-1">Data Source</label>
          <input

            onChange={(e) => setDataSource(e.target.value)}

To achieve this, you can use an animated background GIF or a JavaScript library to create the neural network effect. Here are the steps to update your `HomePage` component to include an animated background:

1. **Find an Animated Background GIF or JavaScript Library**:
   - You can search for a neural network animation GIF online or use a JavaScript library like `particles.js` or `canvas-nest.js` to create the effect.

2. **Add the Background to Your HomePage**:
   - Update the `HomePage` component to include the background animation.

Here is an example using a GIF:

### Step 1: Download a Neural Network Animation GIF
- Download a GIF file and place it in your `public` directory, e.g., `public/neural-network.gif`.

### Step 2: Update `HomePage` Component

```jsx

  return (

      {/* Background GIF */}

        <img
          src="/neural-network.gif"
          alt="Neural Network Animation"

        />

      {/* Content */}

          This application allows you to train a DRL model, test it, and run paper trading.
        </p>
        <ul className="space-y-2 list-none">
          <li>
            <Link href="/train" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Training Form
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/test" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Testing Form
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/paper-trading" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">

            </Link>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Step 3: Update CSS for Better Visibility
- Ensure the text is visible over the background by adding appropriate styles.

### Step 4: Test the Changes
- Run your application and navigate to the home page to see the animated background.

If you prefer using a JavaScript library like `particles.js` or `canvas-nest.js`, you can follow their documentation to integrate the library and create the neural network effect. Here is an example using `canvas-nest.js`:

### Step 1: Install `canvas-nest.js`
```bash
npm install canvas-nest.js
```

### Step 2: Update `HomePage` Component

```jsx
"use client"; // If you plan to use React hooks here. Otherwise, you can omit it if purely static.

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import CanvasNest from "canvas-nest.js";

export default function HomePage() {
  useEffect(() => {
    new CanvasNest(document.querySelector(".background"), {
      color: '0,0,0',
      count: 88,
      opacity: 0.5,
      zIndex: -1,
    });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative min-h-screen bg-black">
      {/* Background Canvas */}
      <div className="background absolute inset-0 z-0"></div>

      {/* Content */}
      <div className="relative z-10 mt-4 text-white">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6">Welcome to CryptoMatic with AI</h1>
        <p className="mb-6">
          This application allows you to train a DRL model, test it, and run paper trading.
        </p>
        <ul className="space-y-2 list-none">
          <li>
            <Link href="/train" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Training Form
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/test" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Testing Form
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/paper-trading" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Paper Trading Form
            </Link>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Step 3: Update CSS for Better Visibility
- Ensure the text is visible over the background by adding appropriate styles.

### Step 4: Test the Changes
- Run your application and navigate to the home page to see the animated background.

The error occurs because `window` is not available during server-side rendering (SSR). To fix this, you need to ensure that `CanvasNest` is only initialized on the client side. You can use dynamic imports with `next/dynamic` to achieve this.

Here is how you can update your `HomePage` component to use `CanvasNest` only on the client side:

### Step 1: Update `HomePage` Component

```jsx
"use client"; // If you plan to use React hooks here. Otherwise, you can omit it if purely static.

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";

// Dynamically import CanvasNest to ensure it runs only on the client side
const CanvasNest = dynamic(() => import("canvas-nest.js"), { ssr: false });

export default function HomePage() {
  useEffect(() => {
    new CanvasNest(document.querySelector(".background"), {
      color: '0,0,0',
      count: 88,
      opacity: 0.5,
      zIndex: -1,
    });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative min-h-screen bg-black">
      {/* Background Canvas */}
      <div className="background absolute inset-0 z-0"></div>

      {/* Content */}
      <div className="relative z-10 mt-4 text-white">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6">Welcome to CryptoMatic with AI</h1>
        <p className="mb-6">
          This application allows you to train a DRL model, test it, and run paper trading.
        </p>
        <ul className="space-y-2 list-none">
          <li>
            <Link href="/train" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Training Form
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/test" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Testing Form
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/paper-trading" className="text-blue-300 hover:underline">
              Go to Paper Trading Form
            </Link>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Step 2: Test the Changes
- Run your application and navigate to the home page to see the animated background.

This approach ensures that `CanvasNest` is only initialized on the client side, avoiding the `window is not defined` error during server-side rendering.
